Results posted for the terminated phase 2 poziotinib programme (n=648) in EGFR/HER2 exon 20 insertion NSCLC, a large dataset for an agent whose development has stalled. https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT03318939
- A Study of TAS3351 in NSCLC Patients With EGFRmt (NCT05765734). Results posted for Taiho's terminated first-in-human study (n=18) of the fourth-generation TKI TAS3351 in acquired EGFR C797S NSCLC, a small early dataset. https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05765734
- Pulmonary Toxicities of Antibody-Drug Conjugates in SCLC and NSCLC: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis. (J Thorac Oncol). PROSPERO-registered meta-analysis of 24 prospective ADC trials (2855 ADC-treated patients) quantifying pulmonary AEs (pneumonitis/ILD 8.0%, grade >=3 2.8%), clinically useful safety synthesis but not new trial results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42674256/
- Efficacy and safety of B7-H3-targeted antibody-drug conjugates in previously treated small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis with a contextual comparison to topotecan. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis of four single-arm B7-H3 ADC studies in pretreated SCLC (pooled ORR 54%, median PFS 5.6 months) with only an external topotecan benchmark, not a randomized comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42661867/
- Liquid biopsy in solid tumours: expert opinion paper of the European society of pathology. (Virchows Arch). European Society of Pathology expert opinion on liquid biopsy implementation, relevant to detecting acquired resistance alterations at progression in NSCLC but not lung-specific clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42658220/
- Zidesamtinib (Jideytro®): a new selective, brain-penetrant ROS1 inhibitor approved for the treatment of ROS1-positive non-small cell lung cancer. (Med Chem Res). Drug profile of zidesamtinib after its July 2026 FDA accelerated approval for TKI-pretreated ROS1-positive NSCLC (including G2032R), a resistance-relevant next-generation agent but outside EGFR and a review rather than primary data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42611059/
- The pan-tumor landscape, allelic status, and genomic complexity of SMARCA4 alterations. (Clin Cancer Res). Large MSK-IMPACT pan-cancer analysis (68,920 samples) of SMARCA4 alteration classes, with a signal of benefit from the BRM degrader PRT3789 in class 2 alterations in a phase 1 trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42599160/
- M1C is a druggable target for NSCLC KRAS G12C mutant tumors resistant to KRAS inhibitors. (Oncogene). Preclinical study showing MUC1-C drives sotorasib resistance and an M1C ADC is active in two sotorasib-resistant PDX models, with a credible clinical path but outside EGFR. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42557306/
- In vitro evaluation of sacituzumab govitecan in non-small cell lung cancer with actionable genomic alterations. (Transl Oncol). In vitro study of sacituzumab govitecan in 16 NSCLC cell lines with actionable alterations showing activity independent of TROP2 level and correlating with SLFN11, preclinical ADC data relevant to driver-positive NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42526386/
- SEZ6-Targeted PET Imaging and Alpha-Particle Radioimmunotherapy is Effective for Detection and Treatment of Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Cancer Res). Preclinical development of SEZ6-targeted PET imaging and alpha-radioimmunotherapy in SCLC models, a credible next-generation approach but no patient data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42526052/
- PF-08046876, a differentiated integrin beta-6 antibody-drug conjugate delivering a potent camptothecin payload optimized for bystander effect and reduced drug efflux. (Mol Cancer Ther). Preclinical characterization of PF-08046876, an integrin beta-6 ADC with an efflux-resistant camptothecin payload, a next-generation ADC in clinical development but without patient data here. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42515977/
- Emrelis for c-Met Positive NSCLC: A Breakthrough in Antibody-Drug Conjugate-based Treatment Approved by the FDA. (Anticancer Agents Med Chem). Drug review of telisotuzumab vedotin (Emrelis), FDA-approved for c-Met-overexpressing NSCLC on the basis of LUMINOSITY ORR 35%, relevant MET-directed ADC but no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42515912/
- Tailored strategies to overcome third-generation TKI resistance in EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer. (Expert Opin Ther Targets). Expert review of tailored strategies after third-generation EGFR-TKI resistance (C797S, MET/HER2 amplification, SCLC transformation, bispecifics and ADCs), directly on topic but without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42473726/
- PRKCI-ECT2 copy number gain promotes developmental reprogramming and metastatic competence in lung adenocarcinoma. (Cell Rep). Mouse-model study showing PRKCI-ECT2 copy-number gain (about 30% of LUAD) drives lineage reprogramming and histological transformation, mechanistic insight into transformation without clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42470637/
- Interleukin-1α-Mediated Activation of TNF-α/NF-κB Signaling Confers Resistance to Osimertinib in EGFR-Mutant Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer. (Thorac Cancer). Cell-line study linking IL-1alpha-driven TNF-alpha/NF-kB signaling in cycling persister cells to osimertinib resistance in PC9/HCC827, preclinical EGFR resistance mechanism. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42469184/
- Multiparatopic antibodies overcome tyrosine kinase inhibitor resistance by inducing lysosomal degradation of EGFR mutants. (Oncogene). Preclinical engineering of multiparatopic EGFR nanobody antibodies that induce lysosomal EGFR degradation and outperform osimertinib in TKI-resistant xenografts, a novel degrader-like strategy without clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42463770/
- PLCG1 and RAC1 orchestrate adaptive cellular programs driving osimertinib-tolerant persister cell formation in malignant pleural effusion-associated non-small cell lung cancer. (Eur J Pharm Sci). Preclinical study showing malignant pleural effusion induces osimertinib-tolerant persister cells via PLCG1 and RAC1 in PC9/H1975 cells. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42463032/
- Design, synthesis, and biological characterization of a hydrophobic tagging EGFR degrader for the treatment of non-small-cell lung cancer. (Mol Divers). Gefitinib-based hydrophobic-tagging EGFR degrader B3 with micromolar DC50 and xenograft activity in EGFR del19 models, an early degrader with in vivo data but not tested in resistant models. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42461464/
- Targeting MET in 2025: From Exon 14 Skipping to MET-Amplified Acquired Resistance in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Int J Mol Sci). Review of MET targeting covering MET amplification as osimertinib resistance with SAVANNAH, SACHI and INSIGHT 2 combination data, on topic but no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42450152/
- Small-cell transformation of advanced non-small-cell lung cancer harboring driver mutations following tyrosine kinase inhibitor therapy: a multicenter real-world study. (J Thorac Dis). Multicenter real-world cohort of 387 driver-mutant NSCLC patients on TKIs (73% EGFR) finding SCLC transformation in 14.3% of re-biopsied patients, mostly after first-line therapy, with shorter survival. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42444874/
- LPIN2 contributes to tyrosine kinase inhibitor resistance via activation of PI3K pathway. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Cell-line study showing LPIN2-mediated triglyceride synthesis activates PI3K signaling in osimertinib- and alectinib-resistant NSCLC cells, a preclinical resistance mechanism. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42433229/
- Combined therapy with ALK inhibitors and a novel EGFR-targeting antibody‒drug conjugate as a strategy for ALK-rearranged non-small cell lung cancers. (Oncogenesis). Preclinical study combining ALK inhibitors with the EGFR-targeting ADC LR004-VC-MMAE in ALK-rearranged NSCLC models, outside EGFR-mutant disease. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42431869/
- Early radiotherapy delays acquired gefitinib resistance in EGFR-mutant NSCLC by suppressing c-FOS-associated survival signaling. (Respir Res). Xenograft and cell study showing early radiotherapy delays gefitinib resistance via c-FOS suppression, mechanistic support for a prior phase II trial in EGFR-mutant NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42426756/
- ANGPTL4 contributes to osimertinib resistance through NDRG1-associated extracellular matrix remodeling in EGFR-mutant NSCLC organoid models. (Cancer Cell Int). Organoid study identifying ANGPTL4-NDRG1 extracellular matrix remodeling as an osimertinib resistance mechanism in EGFR-mutant NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42415069/
- ULK1, a novel therapeutic target to delay drug tolerance to EGFR-TKIs in an EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer model. (Lung Cancer). Preclinical study showing ULK1 inhibition delays osimertinib drug-tolerant persister emergence in EGFR-mutant NSCLC cells. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42407241/
- Amphiregulin-mediated EGFR activation drives both intrinsic and acquired resistance to KRAS G12C inhibitors in KRAS G12C-mutant non-small cell lung cancer. (Oncogene). PDX and mouse study showing autocrine amphiregulin-EGFR activation drives intrinsic and acquired KRAS G12C inhibitor resistance (including CNS), with KRAS plus EGFR inhibition overcoming it and AREG high in 3 of 9 patient tumors. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42393288/
- First-line osimertinib in advanced EGFR-mutated NSCLC: real-world outcomes, clinicogenomic correlates, and oligoprogression management in a multicenter Spanish cohort. (ESMO Real World Data Digit Oncol). Multicenter Spanish real-world cohort of 268 patients on first-line osimertinib (ORR 73.5%, rwPFS 17.5 and rwOS 28.1 months, only 50% reaching second line), with oligoprogression and re-biopsy data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42383195/
- MET Dependence Oversteps EGFR Dependence via Balancing Dimerization of the Receptor Tyrosine Kinases in Osimertinib-Resistant MET-Amplified, EGFR-Mutated Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Thorac Cancer). Cell-line study (HCC827OR) showing MET dependence overrides EGFR dependence via receptor dimerization in osimertinib-resistant MET-amplified EGFR-mutant NSCLC, with savolitinib sensitivity. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42381268/
- Coordinated DNA 5-mC and RNA m5C methylation epigenetically regulates MZF1 splice variants to drive EGFR-TKI resistance. (Exp Mol Med). Mechanistic study linking NSUN7/YBX1 RNA m5C methylation to MZF1 splice switching and EGFR-TKI resistance, with MZF1S upregulated in resistant patients. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42380284/
- TRMT1-mediated tRNA m22G modification drives Osimertinib resistance via the ATXN3/USP25 axis in lung adenocarcinoma. (Cell Death Dis). Mechanistic study identifying an ATXN3-USP25-TRMT1 tRNA modification axis driving osimertinib resistance, reversed by the USP25 inhibitor AZ1 in patient-derived organoids. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42373595/
- Targeting the TBK1-p62 condensate axis restores sensitivity to EGFR-TKIs in resistant lung cancer. (Protein Cell). Preclinical study showing TBK1-driven p62 condensates promote EGFR-TKI resistance, with xenograft validation of restored sensitivity. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42360721/
- Evaluating datopotamab deruxtecan (Dato-DXd) as a novel treatment option for EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer. (Future Oncol). Drug review of Dato-DXd in EGFR-mutated NSCLC summarizing pooled TROPION-Lung01/05 data (ORR about 45%, DOR 6.5 months) and ORCHARD, on topic but no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42345080/
- Brief Report: Proposed Clinical Trial Guidelines Based on Clinicopathologic Heterogeneity of Metastatic Pulmonary Large Cell Neuroendocrine Carcinoma. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO retrospective series of 137 metastatic LCNEC patients (15 transformed) showing poor OS regardless of NSCLC- vs SCLC-like regimens, used to propose trial guidelines. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42341877/
- [Consensus of Chinese experts on the multidisciplinary management of adverse reactions to antibody-drug conjugates in the treatment of lung cancer (2025 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus (2025) on multidisciplinary management of ADC adverse reactions in lung cancer, practical guidance but no new data and regional scope. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42331546/
- Sex-based differences in tolerability of developmental antibody-drug conjugates (ADCs) in non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC). (ESMO Open). Single-center cohort of 132 NSCLC patients on developmental ADCs showing women had more grade >=2 TRAEs (82.8% vs 69.1%, adjusted OR 2.8) and dose reductions. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42330841/
- Cost-effectiveness analysis of sacituzumab tirumotecan as second-line therapy for EGFR-TKI-resistant, EGFR-mutated advanced NSCLC in China. (Expert Rev Pharmacoecon Outcomes Res). Chinese cost-effectiveness model of sac-TMT vs chemotherapy after EGFR-TKI resistance based on OptiTROP-Lung04, ICER $75,704/QALY above threshold, relevant to access. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42324853/
- Characteristics and Clinical Outcomes of De Novo and Acquired Uncommon Compound EGFR Mutations in Patients With Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Brief Report. (Clin Lung Cancer). Institutional and GENIE retrospective of compound EGFR mutations showing acquired C797S more frequent with exon19del after osimertinib (20% vs 7%) and variable osimertinib TTF by compound subtype. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42320198/
- Real-World Patient Characteristics, Mutational Landscape, and Outcomes in Advanced/Metastatic HER2-Mutant Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JCO Precis Oncol). Flatiron-FMI real-world study of 262 oncogenic HER2-mutant NSCLC patients showing poor OS with standard first/second-line therapy (13.5 and 11.1 months). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42314092/
- Clinicogenomic analysis of EGFR-mutant lung tumors identifies Rb pathway inactivation as a hallmark of squamous transformation. (Sci Transl Med). Clinicogenomic and model study identifying Rb pathway/CDKN2A loss as a driver of squamous transformation in EGFR-mutant LUAD, with MET upregulation and EGFR+MET inhibition active in PDX models; translational rather than clinical outcome data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42308331/
- Dacomitinib for EGFR-L858R-mutated non-small cell lung cancer following acquired resistance to third-generation EGFR-TKIs: a retrospective real-world study. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Single-center retrospective of 42 EGFR L858R patients receiving dacomitinib after third-generation TKI resistance (ORR 33.3%, median PFS 5.7 months, iPFS 12.9 months), post-osimertinib clinical data but small and uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42291371/
- RRBP1 promotes osimertinib resistance by activating fatty acid oxidation-dependent metabolic and immune reprogramming in EGFR-Mutant lung adenocarcinoma. (Biochem Pharmacol). Multi-omic and functional study identifying RRBP1-driven fatty acid oxidation as an osimertinib resistance mechanism in EGFR-mutant LUAD. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42285369/
- Tumor cell-intrinsic NSUN2 deficiency reprograms macrophages to sensitize non-small cell lung cancer to EGFR inhibitors by reversing immune evasion. (Neoplasia). Preclinical study showing NSUN2 knockdown restores gefitinib/osimertinib sensitivity only in immunocompetent models by repolarizing macrophages. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42284680/
- Dual actionability of BMI1 activation and mitotic vulnerability defines adaptive Osimertinib resistance in EGFR-mutant NSCLC. (Cell Death Dis). Cell-line study linking BMI1 activation and mitotic vulnerability to adaptive osimertinib resistance in H1975 cells. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42270590/
- Real-world clinical characteristics and outcomes in patients with HER2-mutant non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) who received second-line treatment: A nationwide database analysis in Japan. (Lung Cancer). Japanese nationwide database of 168 HER2-mutant NSCLC patients receiving second-line therapy, showing poor outcomes (median ToT 4.8 months, ORR 33.1%). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42263332/
- Consistency of c-Met protein overexpression over time in patients with non-squamous non-small cell lung cancer. (Histopathology). Retrospective study of 160 patients showing 81.3% concordance of c-Met overexpression over time but only 65.8% in EGFR-mutant patients, relevant to retesting for MET ADCs after targeted therapy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42262061/
- GPNMB-ECD drives acquired resistance to osimertinib in NSCLC via inducing tumor cell cytoskeletal reorganization. (Cell Mol Biol Lett). Study implicating soluble GPNMB-ECD in acquired osimertinib resistance via cytoskeletal reorganization, using patient tissue/plasma and cell models. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42260371/
- Retrospective Analysis of HER2 Testing, Treatment Patterns, and Clinical Outcomes in Patients With Locally Advanced or Metastatic NSCLC With HER2 Mutations in France. (Cancer Med). French ESME database study of HER2 testing frequency, treatment patterns and outcomes in HER2-mutant advanced NSCLC, real-world access data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42252502/
- Enhanced Efficacy of EGFR-Targeted ADCs via p38-Mediated Noncanonical Endocytosis in EGFR-Mutant Lung Cancer Cells. (Biol Pharm Bull). Cell study showing p38-mediated noncanonical EGFR endocytosis enhances cetuximab-MMAE ADC uptake in EGFR-mutant PC-9 cells, with cisplatin activating the pathway. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42252196/
- "Outstanding" ADC Candidates Emerge for SCLC, Other Cancers. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery news item summarizing early phase 1 response data for three SCLC ADCs (DXC006, BL-M14D1, SYS6043), not a primary report. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42249527/
- Evolution and recent advances in antibody-drug conjugate therapy for lung cancer: a comprehensive systematic review based on the Trialtrove database (inception to July 1, 2025). (Front Oncol). Systematic review of 466 registered ADC trials in lung cancer from Trialtrove, describing the trial landscape rather than efficacy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42245686/
- Paired ctDNA analysis reveals diverse resistance mechanisms to mobocertinib in EGFR exon 20 insertion NSCLC. (Front Oncol). Prospective single-center study of 22 EGFR exon 20 insertion patients on mobocertinib (ORR 59%, PFS 5.6 months) with paired ctDNA showing diverse resistance including SCLC transformation; small sample. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42239888/
- Landscape of Chinese Lung Cancer Patients With Compound Mutations and Acquired Resistance Alterations: A Retrospective Study. (Int J Cancer). Large Chinese NGS cohort (8849 patients) characterizing compound EGFR mutations, with 77 paired samples showing T790M (71%) and RAS/MAPK (18%) as acquired resistance mechanisms. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42237450/
- Afatinib Overcomes Osimertinib Resistance via Egfr V804F Mutation in a Syngeneic Egfr-Mutant Lung Cancer Mouse Model. (Cancer Sci). Syngeneic mouse model identifying on-target Egfr V804F (human V802F) as an osimertinib resistance mutation that remains sensitive to afatinib in vivo. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42226508/
- PD-(L)1 containing rechallenge strategies in patients with advanced NSCLC previously treated with immunotherapy: A systematic review and meta-analysis across resistance phenotypes. (Cancer Treat Rev). Meta-analysis of 10 RCTs (n=3,081) finding PD-(L)1 rechallenge gives modest OS benefit (HR 0.91), mainly in acquired-resistance phenotypes; immunotherapy resistance rather than EGFR/ADC focus. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42224849/
- Multifactorial sheltering in peristromal niches shapes in vivo responses of lung cancers to targeted therapies. (Nat Commun). Preclinical ALK+ NSCLC models show peristromal niches shelter residual cells and that adaptive HER2 upregulation can be exploited with T-DXd to suppress relapse, a credible ADC-based strategy against residual disease. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42215447/
- H4K8 lactylation-mediated ferroptosis defense drives EGFR-TKI resistance in lung adenocarcinoma. (Apoptosis). Preclinical study with xenograft validation linking H4K8 lactylation-driven AKR1C1 ferroptosis defence to EGFR-TKI resistance, a mechanistic target without clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42213229/
- Targeted KRASG12V Degradation In Vivo Elicits Lung Adenocarcinoma Regression with Subsequent Relapse from Dysregulated Proteolysis. (Cancer Res). In vivo mouse model showing KRAS G12V PROTAC degradation induces LUAD regression with relapse via proteolysis machinery dysregulation; preclinical degrader data outside EGFR. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42200804/
- STRIP2 confers acquired resistance to EGFR-TKIs in lung adenocarcinoma by modulating IGF2BP3-dependent Twist1 stability. (Genomics). Preclinical in vitro and in vivo work identifying a STRIP2-IGF2BP3-Twist1 m6A axis driving acquired EGFR-TKI resistance, without clinical validation beyond tissue expression. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42191053/
- Silencing of NUP62 overcomes osimertinib resistance via ubiquitination of survivin in non-small cell lung cancer cells. (Br J Cancer). Preclinical cell-line and xenograft study showing NUP62 knockdown restores osimertinib sensitivity via survivin ubiquitination; siRNA approach far from the clinic. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42185638/
- An HDAC4-specific PROTAC degrader achieves radiation sensitization by enhancing ferroptosis in lung cancer. (Nat Commun). Nature Communications preclinical study of an HDAC4-specific PROTAC (TP1) that radiosensitises lung cancer organoids and xenografts via ferroptosis; degrader with in vivo data but not EGFR-directed. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42185286/
- TROPION-Lung14 study protocol: a phase III study of osimertinib in combination with datopotamab deruxtecan versus osimertinib alone as first-line treatment for patients with EGFR-mutated locally advanced or metastatic non-small cell lung cancer. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Protocol paper for phase III TROPION-Lung14 (osimertinib plus Dato-DXd vs osimertinib, ~562 patients, first-line EGFR-mutant NSCLC); important design but no results yet. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42182601/
- Cost-effectiveness analysis based on two models of sacituzumab tirumotecan versus platinum-based chemotherapy as second-line treatment in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer. (Front Public Health). Chinese cost-effectiveness model of sac-TMT versus chemotherapy after EGFR-TKI failure (from OptiTROP-Lung04) finding ICERs above willingness-to-pay; access-relevant but modelling only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42180459/
- Comprehensive characterization of MET exon 14 skipping mutations in non-small cell lung cancer. (Respir Res). Genomic characterisation of 585 METex14 NSCLC patients including 16 resistant to EGFR/ALK inhibitors, describing co-alterations linked to worse crizotinib outcomes; descriptive panel data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42174600/
- Overcoming Primary and Acquired Resistance to Immunotherapy in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Mechanisms, Challenges, and Emerging Strategies. (J Clin Oncol). JCO review of mechanisms and emerging strategies for immunotherapy resistance in NSCLC; a top-journal review without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42172565/
- Pre-clinical efficacy of trastuzumab deruxtecan plus sotorasib in KRASG12C -mutant non-small cell lung cancer and exploratory HER2 profiling in human tumors. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Preclinical xenograft study showing sotorasib plus T-DXd yields deeper, durable regressions in KRAS G12C NSCLC including sotorasib-relapsed models, with exploratory HER2 IHC in 31 patients. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42170277/
- Light-controlled disruption of cancer cell dormancy via photoswitchable stress hormone receptor degraders. (Proc Natl Acad Sci U S A). Preclinical chemistry study of photoswitchable glucocorticoid-receptor photoPROTACs disrupting dormancy programs in NSCLC cells; early-stage degrader concept without in vivo efficacy shown in abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42166243/
- ARIAN protocol: a phase III study of sacituzumab and zimberelimab for the treatment of patients with non-small cell lung cancer. (Expert Opin Biol Ther). Protocol of the phase III ARIAN trial randomising non-pCR resected NSCLC to observation, zimberelimab, or sacituzumab govitecan plus zimberelimab; an ADC trial design without results and not EGFR-focused. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42159280/
- Pan-cancer analysis in the real-world setting uncovers immunogenomic drivers of acquired resistance post-immunotherapy. (J Immunother Cancer). Large real-world pan-cancer clinicogenomic analysis (>5,000 patients) of acquired versus primary ICB resistance, confirming B2M loss in NSCLC; immunotherapy rather than EGFR/ADC resistance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42156138/
- Convergent targeting of FUNDC1-dependent mitophagy sensitises and overcomes resistance to EGFR inhibition. (Clin Transl Med). Preclinical study showing nitidine (from a traditional formulation) synergises with osimertinib by disrupting FUNDC1 mitophagy and targets osimertinib-resistant cells; mechanistic, cell and model data only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42152723/
- CAMK2D isoform 15 facilitates gefitinib resistance via AKT phosphorylation in lung adenocarcinoma. (Cancer Biol Med). Preclinical study with clinical specimens and xenografts linking CAMK2D isoform 15 to gefitinib resistance via AKT; first-generation TKI context limits clinical relevance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42152470/
- Multispecific nanobody degraders co-deplete membrane receptors and enable targeted delivery of diverse payloads. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) describing a trispecific EGFR/cMET/TfR1 nanobody degrader with picomolar receptor depletion and MMAE conjugation in NSCLC models; early preclinical platform. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42146646/
- Development of SDP0505: a first-in-class HER3 × c-Met bispecific ADC, demonstrates potent antitumor activity in EGFR TKI-resistant NSCLC, CRC, and beyond. (Antib Ther). Preclinical development of SDP0505, a HER3 x c-Met bispecific ADC with superior activity in EGFR-TKI-resistant PDX models and acceptable primate toxicology, supporting a phase I trial in China. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42145655/
- TP63 drives TKI resistance in EGFR-mutant lung cancer via ferroptosis inhibition. (Chin Clin Oncol). Preclinical study in osimertinib-resistant PC9/HCC827 cells linking TP63 to adaptive EGFR-TKI resistance via ferroptosis inhibition, in vitro only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42145014/
- Antibody-Mediated Delivery of BRM/BRG1 Protein Degraders Affords Strong Antitumor Efficacy in Multiple BRM-Dependent Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Xenograft Models. (J Med Chem). Medicinal chemistry study of Trop2- and CD71-targeted degrader-antibody conjugates delivering a BRM PROTAC with antigen-dependent efficacy in NSCLC xenografts; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42144767/
- USP48 stabilizes YBX1 to enforce a Wnt-driven stemness circuit and osimertinib resistance in EGFR-mutant NSCLC. (Cancer Lett). Preclinical CRISPR screen in patient-derived organoids identifying a USP48-YBX1-Wnt stemness loop sustaining osimertinib resistance in EGFR-mutant NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42142703/
- Pattern of resistance on first-line EGFR-directed therapy in EGFR-positive metastatic NSCLC. (Ecancermedicalscience). Post hoc analysis of a 350-patient Indian phase III trial of gefitinib with or without chemotherapy showing lower T790M emergence with chemotherapy (19% vs 37%) and 6% SCLC transformation; first-generation TKI setting. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42131879/
- The novel ALK K1150dup mutation mediates resistance to frontline lorlatinib and retains sensitivity to gilteritinib. (NPJ Precis Oncol). Identifies a novel ALK K1150dup mutation causing resistance to first-line lorlatinib and neladalkib in Ba/F3 cells with sensitivity to gilteritinib; single-patient origin, ALK rather than EGFR. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42129432/
- mSWI/SNF complex inhibition sensitizes KRAS-mutant lung cancers to targeted therapies via epithelial-mesenchymal subversion. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) showing SMARCA4/2 inhibitor FHD-286 resensitises KRAS-mutant NSCLC to KRAS inhibitors in organoids and PDX models; preclinical, non-EGFR. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42124568/
- TGM2-TUFM promotes acquired resistance to EGFR-TKIs in EGFR-mutated NSCLC through p62-mediated lipophagy. (Respir Res). Preclinical study with xenografts and tissue samples showing TGM2-TUFM drives EGFR-TKI resistance via p62-mediated lipophagy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42106683/
- Glypican-1-targeted antibody-drug conjugate shows therapeutic efficacy in lung squamous cell carcinoma. (Lung Cancer). Preclinical glypican-1 ADC showing efficacy in LUSC cell lines and subcutaneous and bone-metastasis xenografts; early ADC target outside EGFR disease. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42096801/
- Genomic characterization of DNA damage response pathway mutations reveals their adverse impact on EGFR-TKI efficacy in non-small cell lung cancer. (Respir Res). Retrospective genomic study of 202 EGFR-mutant patients (plus 126 GENIE validation) linking DDR pathway mutations to shorter first-line EGFR-TKI PFS, especially with TP53 co-mutation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42092972/
- Design, synthesis, and biological evaluation of novel probe-quality EGFR degraders targeting wild-type and Del19 mutation. (Bioorg Med Chem Lett). Medicinal chemistry study of allosteric EGFR PROTACs degrading Del19 and L858R/T790M EGFR in cells, with weak antiproliferative potency and no in vivo data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42092430/
- Unmasking the common enemy: drug resistance mechanisms across three different EGFR inhibitor generations are associated with co-targetable alterations in extracellular matrix signaling. (Cell Commun Signal). Proteomic study of cell lines made resistant to gefitinib, dacomitinib and osimertinib identifying shared, co-targetable FN1/FAK/YAP1 extracellular-matrix drivers; in vitro with public-dataset correlation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42087187/
- Emerging Targeted Therapies for HER2-Mutant NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of HER2-directed ADCs and selective TKIs (zongertinib, sevabertinib) in HER2-mutant NSCLC; top-journal review without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42082076/
- Copy Number Amplification and c-Myc Transcriptional Activation-Mediated RNA-Binding Protein MEX3A Promotes EGFR-TKI Resistance in Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer. (Thorac Cancer). Paired pre/post-TKI tumours from 18 patients plus xenografts implicate amplified, c-Myc-driven MEX3A in EGFR-TKI resistance; preclinical mechanism with small clinical series. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42080737/
- Acquired resistance to the PRMT5 inhibitor confers collateral sensitivity to MEK inhibition in MTAP-null non-small cell lung cancer. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) showing acquired resistance to an MTA-cooperative PRMT5 inhibitor in MTAP-null NSCLC confers collateral MEK-inhibitor sensitivity; in vitro only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42079286/
- Bidirectional coupling among EMT, AXL-RB1 signaling and lineage switch drives resistance to osimertinib and worse clinical outcomes in NSCLC. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) systems-modelling study linking EMT, AXL-RB1 signalling and lineage switch to osimertinib resistance, validated in transcriptomic datasets. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42079213/
- Characterization and therapeutic suppression of KEAP1-NRF2-driven resistance to KRAS inhibitors in pancreatic and lung cancer. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) CRISPR screen identifying KEAP1 loss as resistance to KRAS G12D/RAS(ON) inhibitors, reversible with a glutamine antagonist in pancreatic and lung tumours. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42079131/
- Clinical Efficacy of HER2-targeted Monotherapy in ERBB2-mutant Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Single-arm Meta-Analysis. (Clin Lung Cancer). Single-arm meta-analysis of 20 studies (n=1,489) of HER2-targeted monotherapy in HER2-mutant NSCLC with pooled ORR 51%; exploratory, heterogeneous, not post-EGFR-TKI. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42070909/
- Design, synthesis and biological evaluation of 2,4,5-trisubstituted 7H-Pyrrolo[2,3-d]pyrimidine derivatives as potent EGFR tyrosine kinase inhibitors against the C797S acquired resistance mutation. (Bioorg Med Chem). Medicinal chemistry study of LN-B72, a pyrrolopyrimidine EGFR inhibitor active against Del19/T790M/C797S and L858R/T790M/C797S (IC50 ~8 nM) with in vivo tumour inhibition; preclinical fourth-generation candidate. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42070540/
- A Retrospective, Multicenter Analysis Within the National Network Genomic Medicine Lung Cancer in Germany to Detect RET Fusions as a Possible Mechanism of Resistance in Patients With EGFR Mutations. (Clin Lung Cancer). German multicentre series of 9 EGFR-mutant patients with RET fusions (7 acquired) including 3 treated with osimertinib plus pralsetinib (PFS 3.9-10.5 months); very small but clinically informative. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42055889/
- Interstitial lung disease associated with novel anticancer agents in non-small cell lung cancer: a pharmacovigilance analysis using the FAERS database. (Ther Adv Respir Dis). FAERS pharmacovigilance analysis of 4,712 NSCLC ILD reports finding signals for ICIs, T-DXd (ROR 3.14) and osimertinib, with ILD mostly in the first month; spontaneous-report data cannot give incidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42047678/
- Stanniocalcin 2-Induced Autophagy Confers Resistance of Lung Cancer Cells to EGFR Inhibition via the ERK/Beclin 1 Signaling. (Cancer Med). Preclinical in vitro and in vivo study showing STC2-induced autophagy via ERK/Beclin 1 confers EGFR-TKI resistance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42043462/
- Immunotherapy for TKI-resistant, EGFR L858R-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized and single-arm studies. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis of 17 studies (1,154 patients) in TKI-resistant EGFR L858R NSCLC finding ICI combinations, not monotherapy, improve PFS versus chemotherapy (HR 0.63) without OS benefit; mixes RCTs and single-arm studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42039162/
- IGF2R as a master regulator of acquired EGFR inhibitor resistance and therapeutic target in non-small cell lung cancer. (Int J Biol Macromol). Preclinical study identifying IGF2R as a mediator of acquired osimertinib resistance with resensitisation upon knockdown in vitro and in vivo. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42035856/
- USP7-dependent stabilization of FKBP4 contributes to acquired osimertinib resistance through glycolytic remodeling in NSCLC. (Sci Rep). Preclinical study showing USP7-stabilised FKBP4 drives acquired osimertinib resistance through glycolytic remodelling and PI3K-AKT signalling. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42031983/
- TRIM26-mediated NKRF degradation drives Osimertinib resistance through SNRPD2-dependent stress granule formation in lung adenocarcinoma. (Cell Death Dis). Preclinical study showing TRIM26-mediated NKRF loss drives osimertinib resistance via SNRPD2-dependent stress granules, with xenograft validation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42026030/
- CZC54252 overcomes Osimertinib resistance by targeting EGFRC797S mutations. (BMC Pharmacol Toxicol). Drug-repurposing screen identifying CZC54252 as active against EGFR del19/L858R-T790M-C797S in engineered PC-9 cells and xenografts; early preclinical C797S candidate. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42021316/
- Mapping Real-world Rebiopsy Events to Treatment Trajectories in EGFR/ALK/ROS1-Driven Non-small-cell Lung Cancer: Insights from the AURORA Cohort. (Clin Lung Cancer). Australian AURORA real-world cohort of 281 EGFR/ALK/ROS1 patients showing rebiopsy at progression rose from 51% to 70% over time; descriptive practice data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42014297/
- Comparative analysis of therapeutic target protein expression in de novo and transformed small cell lung carcinomas using paired biopsy samples. (Lung Cancer). Paired-biopsy IHC study of 15 SCLC patients (6 transformed) showing DLL3 rose in all transformed cases while TROP2 and HER2 fell, relevant to ADC/BiTE selection after transformation; very small. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42013561/
- Matched targeted therapy use after broad genomic profiling in advanced Non-Small cell lung cancer. (J Natl Cancer Inst). Large US real-world cohort (n=6,620) showing underuse of guideline-concordant targeted therapy after genomic profiling, especially for later-line (33%) and off-label (72%) options. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42679379/
- Safety Evaluation of Tarlatamab in SCLC: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Meta-analysis of 4 tarlatamab trials (457 patients) pooling grade 3-4 TRAEs (42%) and CRS (56%) in SCLC; small number of studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42668666/
- Efficacy and Safety of Durvalumab and Tremelimumab in Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials. (Chronic Dis Transl Med). Meta-analysis of 3 RCTs (n=1,859) finding durvalumab plus tremelimumab improved OS versus chemotherapy in PD-L1 <1% metastatic NSCLC (HR 0.81). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42662437/
- Efficacy and safety of durvalumab with or without tremelimumab in the treatment of metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: a meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (Proc (Bayl Univ Med Cent)). Meta-analysis of MYSTIC, ARCTIC and POSEIDON (n=2,726) finding no OS benefit of adding tremelimumab to durvalumab (HR 0.97) with more immune-related adverse events. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42658167/
- Single- Versus Multi-Fraction Stereotactic Body Radiotherapy for Primary Lung Cancer and Pulmonary Oligometastases: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analyses. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Systematic review and meta-analysis finding similar 2-year local failure for single- versus multi-fraction SBRT at BED10 >=100 Gy in early NSCLC, limited by selection bias. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42657018/
- Preoperative exercise-based prehabilitation for reducing postoperative pulmonary complications after lung cancer surgery: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Front Med (Lausanne)). Meta-analysis of 29 RCTs (n=2,900) finding preoperative exercise prehabilitation reduced postoperative pulmonary complications (RR 0.42) and hospital stay by ~2 days after lung cancer surgery, with moderate-to-very-low certainty. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42656529/
- Association Between Mutations in DNA Damage Response Genes and Efficacy of Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor Therapy in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (Cancers (Basel)). Meta-analysis of 13 mostly retrospective studies (n=5,989) associating DDR mutations with better ICI response and survival in NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42649868/
- AdvanTIG-301: a phase III study of ociperlimab plus tislelizumab and concurrent chemoradiotherapy in stage III unresectable non-small cell lung cancer. (J Immunother Cancer). Phase III AdvanTIG-301 terminated early after randomising only 63 patients to ociperlimab plus tislelizumab, tislelizumab, or durvalumab after cCRT in stage III NSCLC, with descriptive efficacy only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42648750/
- Safety and efficacy of first-line iparomlimab and tuvonralimab (QL1706) plus carboplatin and etoposide in patients with extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: an open-label, single-arm, phase 2 trial (DUBHE-L-209). (Front Immunol). Single-arm phase 2 (n=40) of first-line PD-1/CTLA-4 MabPair QL1706 plus carboplatin-etoposide in ES-SCLC with ORR 92.1%, mPFS 6.0 and mOS 15.8 months. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42643500/
- Quality of life changes following treatment in patients with NSCLC: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Cancer Treat Res Commun). Meta-analysis of 47 studies (7,518 patients) of HRQoL change after NSCLC treatments, showing improvements with targeted therapy and deterioration after surgery and chemotherapy; mixed study designs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42641462/
- Diagnostic value of surveillance 18F-FDG PET/CT for detecting recurrent non-small cell lung cancer after curative resection: a multicentre retrospective cohort study. (Eur J Nucl Med Mol Imaging). Multicentre retrospective study of 6,789 surveillance PET/CT scans in 3,671 resected NSCLC patients reporting 89% sensitivity and 98% specificity for recurrence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42637975/
- Epidemiological characteristics and risk factors of lung cancer in a population with incidental pulmonary nodules in China: a prospective multicenter study of 10,560 cases. (J Natl Cancer Cent). Prospective multicentre cohort of 10,560 people with incidental nodules in China reporting baseline epidemiology and lung cancer risk factors, notably many non-smoking women; baseline-only report. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42630839/
- Neoadjuvant versus perioperative immunotherapy in resectable NSCLC: A PD-L1-stratified Bayesian network meta-analysis. (Front Oncol). Bayesian network meta-analysis of 8 RCTs (3,608 patients) comparing neoadjuvant and perioperative immunochemotherapy by PD-L1 subgroup, limited by indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42625644/
- Spatially Fractionated Induction Radiotherapy to Widen the Therapeutic Window in Locally Advanced Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer (SPARROW): Study Protocol of a Multicenter, Randomized, Open-Label Seamless Phase I/II Trial. (Clin Lung Cancer). Protocol of the SPARROW seamless phase I/II randomised trial of spatially fractionated induction radiotherapy in locally advanced NSCLC (100 patients in phase II). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42623891/
- Increasing lung cancer screening referral/order and completion rates in primary care through patient outreach and shared decision making. (J Med Screen). Randomised study (n=2,572) of telephone outreach and decision counselling that increased screening referral/order rates (16% vs 9%) with a borderline effect on completion. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42623060/
- Tumor-hepatocyte crosstalk drives a hepatic lactate-TGF-β axis of CD8 + T cell exhaustion and immunotherapy resistance in small-cell lung cancer liver metastases. (bioRxiv). Preprint (not peer reviewed) showing a hepatocyte-driven lactate-TGF-beta axis exhausting CD8 T cells in SCLC liver metastases, mechanistic with rapid-autopsy samples. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42619890/
- Increasing uptake of lung cancer screening through a patient 'nudge': A randomized pragmatic trial. (Am J Prev Med). Pragmatic randomised trial (n=894) finding a pre-visit patient nudge letter did not increase lung cancer screening LDCT orders (15% vs 18%). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42617889/
- The clinical value of adding immune checkpoint inhibitors to radiotherapy for cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Ann Med). Meta-analysis of 15 RCTs (7,947 patients) across cancers finding adding ICIs to radiotherapy improved OS and PFS, with the largest effect in NSCLC (OS HR 0.54). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42613912/
- A phase 2 study of encorafenib in combination with binimetinib for Chinese participants with BRAFV600E mutated metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: Results from the OCEAN II study. (Lung Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 OCEAN II in 63 Chinese patients with BRAF V600E NSCLC: encorafenib plus binimetinib achieved ORR 61%, mPFS 13.8 months, consistent with prior global data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42612509/
- DNA hypomethylation identifying clinical benefit subgroup of small-cell lung cancer: multi-omics analysis of a phase II trial with durvalumab plus olaparib as maintenance therapy. (J Immunother Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 (n=60) of durvalumab-chemotherapy then durvalumab plus olaparib maintenance in ES-SCLC (ORR 73.3%, mPFS 6.8, mOS 14.6 months) with a hypomethylation subgroup linked to benefit. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42601174/
- Effectiveness of a symptom network-based core symptom management program on symptom burden in lung cancer patients: A randomized controlled trial. (Eur J Oncol Nurs). Single-centre RCT (n=114) of a symptom network-based management programme after lung cancer surgery reducing symptom burden and improving self-efficacy and QoL at 1 month. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42600403/
- A meta-analysis of cisplatin-based versus carboplatin-based adjuvant chemotherapy for completely resected non-small cell lung cancer. (Gen Thorac Cardiovasc Surg). Meta-analysis of 8 studies (2,269 patients) finding no OS or RFS difference between cisplatin- and carboplatin-based adjuvant chemotherapy, including an RCT-only sensitivity analysis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42599571/
- Efficacy of nab-paclitaxel combined with immune checkpoint inhibitors in solid tumors: a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (Transl Cancer Res). Meta-analysis of 24 RCTs of nab-paclitaxel plus ICIs vs nab-paclitaxel regimens across solid tumours, with lung cancer only one subgroup. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42591455/
- Robotic vs. video-assisted thoracoscopic surgery for resectable non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (J Thorac Dis). Meta-analysis of five RCTs (712 patients, incl. RVlob and RAVAL) comparing robotic vs VATS resection for NSCLC, showing less blood loss and more nodes with RATS. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42583305/
- Stage shift, histological differentiation, and survival patterns of lung squamous cell carcinoma versus adenocarcinoma in low-dose CT screening. (Cancer Epidemiol). Secondary analysis of the NLST randomized screening trial showing LDCT survival benefit among diagnosed cases for adenocarcinoma (aHR 0.54) but not squamous carcinoma (aHR 1.04). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42580153/
- First-line systemic treatment for people with extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: a network meta-analysis. (Cochrane Database Syst Rev). Cochrane network meta-analysis of 14 RCTs (7541 patients) of first-line ES-SCLC regimens, ranking PE plus ICIs (e.g. benmelstobart+anlotinib HR 0.61) against PE alone; a rigorous synthesis of existing trials rather than new trial data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42578486/
- Durvalumab Consolidation After Chemoradiotherapy in Unresectable Stage III Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Efficacy and Safety. (Am J Clin Oncol). Meta-analysis pooling RCT and real-world data on durvalumab consolidation after CRT in unresectable stage III NSCLC, mixing trial and observational evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42574292/
- Rilvegostomig for Metastatic Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A First-In-Human Phase I/II Clinical Study. (Clin Cancer Res). First-in-human phase I/II of the PD-1/TIGIT bispecific rilvegostomig in CPI-pretreated PD-L1+ NSCLC (n=83) showing tolerability but only 5.6% ORR and 3.8-month PFS at RP2D. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42574065/
- Liquid Biopsies for Cancer: A Translational Science Review. (JAMA). JAMA translational review of ctDNA liquid biopsy covering NSCLC genotyping and MRD; a review without new trial data but in a major journal. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42574031/
- Lung Cancer Patient Advocacy Progress and New Challenges Since 2006. (J Thorac Oncol). J Thorac Oncol review of lung cancer patient advocacy since 2006; meets the journal floor but reports no trial or mechanistic data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42552032/
- The effect of motivational interviewing on smoking cessation intention in patients with lung cancer: A randomized controlled trial. (Tob Induc Dis). Small RCT (21 vs 21) of WhatsApp-delivered motivational interviewing to increase smoking cessation intention in lung cancer patients. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42569500/
- Efficacy and safety of first-line immune checkpoint inhibitors combinations for extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: A systematic review and network meta-analysis. (Medicine (Baltimore)). Network meta-analysis of RCTs of first-line ICI combinations in ES-SCLC (search to Oct 2024), a synthesis of existing trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42566592/
- Cost-effectiveness analysis of combined immunotherapy regimens for non-squamous non-small lung cancer after tyrosine kinase inhibitors resistance. (Front Public Health). Network meta-analysis plus Chinese cost-effectiveness model of ivonescimab-, sintilimab- and atezolizumab-based regimens after EGFR-TKI resistance, finding none cost-effective at current prices. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42564047/
- Efficacy and safety of immunotherapy combined with chemoradiotherapy in limited-stage small cell lung cancer: a systematic evaluation and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis of nine RCTs (1853 patients) finding added immunotherapy to chemoradiotherapy improves PFS/OS in LS-SCLC (OS HR 0.85). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42558713/
- CHIO3: CHemotherapy Combined with Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor for Operable Stage IIIA/B (N2) Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Surgical Outcomes. (Ann Thorac Surg). Single-arm phase II CHIO3 trial (n=37) reporting surgical outcomes after neoadjuvant chemo-durvalumab in N2 stage III NSCLC (R0 93%, no 90-day mortality). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42556446/
- Efficacy and safety of immunotherapy in driver mutation-negative advanced non-small cell lung cancer: A systematic review and meta-analysis. (Indian J Med Res). PROSPERO-registered meta-analysis of RCTs of immunotherapy vs chemotherapy in driver-negative advanced NSCLC, confirming established evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42555694/
- [Expert consensus on pathological image annotation for primary bronchial and lung cancer]. (Zhonghua Bing Li Xue Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus on pathological image annotation for primary lung cancer, a consensus statement but focused on annotation methodology rather than treatment. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42547417/
- Continued Cemiplimab Beyond Progression With Addition of Chemotherapy in Patients With Advanced NSCLC With PD-L1 Expression of 50% or Higher: 5-Year Follow-Up From EMPOWER-Lung 1. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Exploratory 5-year analysis of 73 patients from phase 3 EMPOWER-Lung 1 continuing cemiplimab beyond progression with added chemotherapy (ORR 27.4%, mPFS 6.4 mo); non-randomized subset. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42529322/
- Efficacy of first-line treatment in driver gene-negative non-small cell lung cancer with liver metastases: a Bayesian network meta-analysis. (Front Immunol). Bayesian network meta-analysis of 20 RCTs in driver-negative NSCLC with liver metastases favouring PD-1 inhibitor plus chemotherapy (OS HR 0.68). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42529281/
- Durvalumab Consolidation Treatment Following Concurrent Chemoradiotherapy in Limited-Stage SCLC: ADRIATIC China Subgroup Analysis. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Prespecified China subgroup (n=95) of phase 3 ADRIATIC showing consistent but imprecise durvalumab benefit in LS-SCLC (OS HR 0.71, 95% CI 0.37-1.37). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42529258/
- Development of an externally controlled trial from a single-arm study to assess first-line immunochemotherapy in advanced large cell neuroendocrine lung carcinomas: a study protocol from the FIRST-NEC GFPC 01-2022 trial. (BMJ Open). Protocol of the ongoing phase II FIRST-NEC trial with external control testing first-line durvalumab plus platinum-etoposide in advanced LCNEC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42526918/
- The Impact of Electronic Health Record Alerts to Correct Smoking History on Lung Cancer Screening: A Randomized Controlled Quality Improvement Initiative. (Chest). Randomized quality-improvement study of EHR alerts to correct smoking history for screening eligibility (no abstract available to assess size or effect). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42526632/
- Validation of the lung immune prognostic index in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: Post hoc analysis of the caspian and IMpower133 phase 3 trials. (Eur J Cancer). Post hoc analysis of CASPIAN and IMpower133 (n=1140) validating LIPI as a prognostic factor in ES-SCLC (OS HR 1.76 poor vs good) without treatment interaction. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42520592/
- Automated measurement of CD8+ T-cell distribution at the tumor epithelial-stromal interface is associated with NSCLC immunotherapy benefit and transcriptomic pathways. (J Pathol Inform). Digital-pathology CD8+ infiltration score trained on the phase 2 POPLAR trial (n=188) associated with atezolizumab benefit, a retrospective biomarker study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42518513/
- The addition of radial endobronchial ultrasound to electromagnetic navigation bronchoscopy: the NEBULA study - a randomised controlled trial. (ERJ Open Res). Multicentre RCT NEBULA (n=183) finding adding radial EBUS to electromagnetic navigation bronchoscopy did not improve diagnostic yield (43.8% vs 45.7%). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42516899/
- Efficacy and safety of necitumumab and pembrolizumab combination therapy in advanced non-small-cell lung cancer with ≥50% PD-L1 expression: A nonrandomized phase II trial. (Clin Cancer Res). Single-arm phase II (n=50) of first-line necitumumab plus pembrolizumab in PD-L1>=50% NSCLC with 76% ORR but 10% grade 3 ILD. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42507540/
- Speeding to a Stop: Postapproval Barriers to Accessing New Lung Cancer Therapies. (J Clin Oncol). JCO article on post-approval barriers to access to new lung cancer therapies (no abstract), meeting the major-journal floor. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42659602/
- Prognostic Significance of Spread Through Air Spaces in Stage Ia/bN0 NSCLC-An Evaluation in the JCOG0802/WJOG4607L Clinical Trial Patients. (J Thorac Oncol). Pathology analysis of 593 tumours from the JCOG0802/WJOG4607L segmentectomy-vs-lobectomy trial showing STAS independently predicts shorter RFS and more local recurrence; post hoc prognostic work. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42600842/
- Tumor Mutational Burden as a Predictor of Immune-Related Adverse Events in Patients With Advanced NSCLC Receiving Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors: A Brief Report. (J Thorac Oncol). Single-centre JTO brief report of 1215 ICI-treated NSCLC patients relating TMB to immune-related adverse events; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42556463/
- Enabling sustainable supply of the essential cancer medicines etoposide and teniposide in yeast. (Science). Science paper reconstructing the podophyllotoxin pathway in yeast for etoposide/teniposide supply, relevant to SCLC drug supply but not clinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42531388/
- Disease Kinetics and Practice Patterns in More Than or Equal to 4-Year Long-Term Beneficiaries of Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors for Advanced NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). Multicentre retrospective JTO study of 295 ICI long-term beneficiaries showing ~88% lung-cancer-specific OS at 8 years and mostly indolent oligo-progression. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42520967/
- Tumor-infiltrating plasma cell profiling after PD-1 blockade reveals tumor-specific antibodies. (Cancer Cell). Cancer Cell mechanistic study cloning tumour-specific antibodies from plasma cells after neoadjuvant PD-1 blockade and testing a derived CAR-T preclinically. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42508408/
- Brief Report: Reducing Lorlatinib-Related Adverse Events by Establishing the Optimal Exposure-Toxicity Threshold in Patients With NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO brief report in two small cohorts (n=46, n=52) linking higher lorlatinib trough levels to severe toxicity, supporting exposure-guided dosing. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42462816/
- Dendritic cells control tertiary lymphoid structure development and maintenance in cancer. (Science). Science mechanistic study showing cDC1 dendritic cells drive tertiary lymphoid structure formation in a mouse NSCLC model; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42462020/
- Comparison of circulating tumor DNA assays for molecular residual disease detection in non-small cell lung cancer. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO head-to-head comparison of three ctDNA MRD assays in 84 resected stage I-III patients favouring a personalized tumour-informed assay (landmark sensitivity 40.9%). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42448176/
- SEZ6-Targeted ADC Shows Preliminary Efficacy in Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery news brief on preliminary efficacy of a SEZ6-targeted ADC in SCLC (no abstract to assess data). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42446513/
- First-Line Chemoimmunotherapy and Chemotherapy Outcomes in Patients With RET Fusion-Positive Lung Cancer in LIBRETTO-431. (J Thorac Oncol). Secondary non-randomized analysis from phase 3 LIBRETTO-431 finding no PFS/OS difference between chemo-immunotherapy (n=83) and chemotherapy (n=19) in RET-fusion NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42431264/
- Weight Gain on Lorlatinib Is Associated With Accumulation of Visceral Adipose Tissue. (J Thorac Oncol). Retrospective JTO body-composition study of 67 ALK+ patients on lorlatinib showing weight gain is mainly visceral adipose tissue. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42419484/
- Lung Transplant for Refractory Lung-Limited Stage IV Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JAMA). JAMA single-centre prospective registry of 17 lung transplants for refractory lung-limited stage IV NSCLC with 100% 1-year survival vs 40.8% on medical management; tiny, non-randomized. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42418196/
- Cytisine for Smoking Cessation Within a Lung Cancer Screening Program: Results From the Italian RISP Trial. (J Thorac Oncol). Non-randomized analysis within the Italian RISP screening trial (10191 enrolled) where cytisine users had 50.1% vs 8.1% quit rates compared with counselling alone; self-selected comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42409118/
- Genomic Landscape and Clinical Impact of MTAP Loss in Driver-Positive NSCLC: Insights From a Large-Scale Real-World Chinese Cohort. (J Thorac Oncol). Large Chinese real-world genomic cohort (6492) linking MTAP loss to worse outcomes on targeted therapy, especially EGFR-mutant patients on 3rd-generation TKIs (173 treated). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42409117/
- Germline Mutations as Risk Factors for Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO meta-analysis of 28 studies estimating 7.9% prevalence of pathogenic germline variants in lung cancer patients. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42409116/
- Interventional Radiology and Thoracic Oncology: 20 Years of Innovation. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO narrative review of 20 years of interventional radiology in thoracic oncology; meets journal floor without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42399004/
- A dietary switch promotes sensory neuron-dependent cancer-associated cachexia. (Science). Science preclinical study showing LKB1-loss lung tumours drive cachexia via local PGE2 and sensory neurons, worsened by high-fat diet; mouse models only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42391376/
- Obesity Promotes Lung Carcinogenesis Through Airway Immune Dysfunction. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO case-control (1170) plus mouse study linking abdominal fat to lung carcinogenesis via airway immune dysfunction; risk mechanism rather than treatment. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42379297/
- Retraction Note: Time-of-day immunochemotherapy in non-small cell lung cancer: a randomized phase 3 trial. (Nat Med). Retraction of a Nature Medicine randomized phase 3 trial of time-of-day immunochemotherapy in NSCLC, important for interpreting that evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42343117/
- ctDNA-Based MRD Detection in Stage III NSCLC Treated With Chemoradiotherapy and Durvalumab. (J Thorac Oncol). Prospective multicentre study (n=84) showing ctDNA MRD during/after durvalumab predicts PFS in stage III NSCLC (HR 2.95). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42336205/
- Adaptive Feedback Signaling Through the FAK/SRC Pathway Promotes KRAS G12C Inhibitor Resistance. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO preclinical study identifying FAK/SRC feedback as a KRAS G12C inhibitor resistance mechanism and combination strategy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42336204/
- Targeting KRAS codon 13 mutations using direct combination approaches in non-small cell lung cancer. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery study characterising KRAS G13X NSCLC in patient cohorts and preclinical combination therapy with a G13C inhibitor. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42329102/
- EML4-ALK mediates resistance to KRAS G12C inhibition and induces an oncogenic dependency by rewiring signaling through the wild-type RAS pathway. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery preclinical study showing acquired EML4-ALK mediates KRAS G12C inhibitor resistance and is targetable with ALK inhibition. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42329099/
- Selective Inhibition of KRASG13C Reveals an Increased Dependence on Wild-Type RAS Isoforms in Codon 13 RAS-Mutant Cancers. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery preclinical study of RAS(ON) G13C inhibitor RMC-8839 revealing wild-type RAS dependence in codon-13 mutant cancers. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42329095/
- Cellular architecture and neighborhood-informed virtual spatial tumor profiling from histopathology. (Cell). Cell AI platform inferring spatial tumour neighbourhoods from H&E across 457 NSCLC patients and evaluated in over 5000, a translational method without treatment data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42302781/
- Electronic cigarette use after smoking cessation and lung cancer risk. (Nat Med). Nature Medicine Korean cohort of 4.5 million ex-smokers associating post-cessation e-cigarette use with higher lung cancer incidence (aHR 1.56); observational prevention data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42260103/
- Advances in Diagnosis-Pulmonology. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of two decades of pulmonology advances in lung cancer diagnosis; journal floor without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42250919/
- The Prognostic Impact of Common Molecular Alterations in Resected Lung Adenocarcinoma and Implications for the Tenth Edition TNM Classification. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO cohort of 5839 resected adenocarcinomas assessing EGFR/KRAS/ALK/TP53 prognostic value for the 10th-edition TNM; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42250800/
- Plasma signals of lung tumor promotion for molecular cancer prevention. (Cell). Cell study of a 14-protein plasma signature predicting lung cancer >5 years ahead, validated in eight cohorts and identifying CANTOS participants benefiting from IL-1beta inhibition; prevention-focused. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42242224/
- PD-(L)1 Inhibitor Monotherapy vs Chemoimmunotherapy for Advanced NSCLC With High PD-L1 Expression: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (JAMA Oncol). JAMA Oncol meta-analysis of 24 phase 3 RCTs suggesting chemoimmunotherapy outperforms PD-(L)1 monotherapy in PD-L1-high NSCLC (OS HR 0.85 in NMA); indirect comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42240993/
- Resistance to Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor Treatment in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Clinical Trials: A Perspective From Lung-MAP Investigators. (J Clin Oncol). Lung-MAP investigators' perspective in JCO on immune checkpoint inhibitor resistance in NSCLC trials; no abstract, commentary-type piece in a top journal. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42234933/
- SARS-CoV-2 mRNA Vaccination Improved Survival in NSCLC Treated With Radiotherapy. (J Thorac Oncol). Real-world cohort of 413 SABR-treated early NSCLC patients associating peri-SABR SARS-CoV-2 mRNA vaccination with better RFS (PSM HR 0.61) and OS, hypothesis-generating only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42229634/
- Machine Learning Assessment of Pathologic Response in Lung Cancer Resections After Neoadjuvant Therapy-IASLC MPR Project. (J Thorac Oncol). IASLC project showing machine-learning assessment of major pathologic response after neoadjuvant therapy correlates strongly with pathologists (r 0.97, kappa 0.82). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42229633/
- Selpercatinib Improves EFS in RET Fusion-Positive NSCLC. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery news item on LIBRETTO-432 adjuvant selpercatinib EFS benefit in RET fusion-positive NSCLC; the primary NEJM paper is captured separately. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42226675/
- International disruptions to cancer diagnosis and stage at presentation during the COVID-19 pandemic in 2020: an International Cancer Benchmarking Partnership (ICBP) population-based study. (Lancet Oncol). ICBP population study of 2.6 million patients in seven countries (including Norway) estimating an 8% deficit of lung cancer diagnoses during April-December 2020. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42225103/
- Rare EGFRm NSCLC: More First-Line Options Emerge. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery news on WU-KONG28 first-line sunvozertinib and updated CHRYSALIS-2 amivantamab-lazertinib data in rare EGFR mutations; secondary reporting of trials covered elsewhere. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42224429/
- Sensitizing Lung Cancer to Immunotherapy with a RAS(ON) Inhibitor Doublet. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery commentary on a preclinical RAS(ON) inhibitor doublet that combined with checkpoint blockade eliminates immune-cold lung tumours in mice. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42220008/
- Thymic Composition Predicts Radiation Pneumonitis in Locally Advanced NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). Analysis of RTOG 0617 (n=453) and MSKCC (n=230) showing CT-derived thymic tissue predicts lower grade 3+ radiation pneumonitis after chemoradiotherapy independent of lung V20. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42214669/
- Nociceptive innervation limits tertiary lymphoid structures to promote lung cancer. (Cell). Cell mouse study showing nociceptive CGRP signalling blocks tertiary lymphoid structures in LUAD and CGRP blockade sensitises tumours to immunotherapy; preclinical only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42161272/
- Biomarker-Based Eligibility for Lung Cancer Screening: Validation of the Protein-Based INTEGRAL-Risk Model. (JAMA). JAMA development and validation (n=3,695) of a 13-protein INTEGRAL-Risk model to select people with smoking history for lung cancer screening. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42149699/
- Multicenter Cohort Study of Original or Substitute Systemic Therapy With or Without Brain Radiotherapy for Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer With Brain-Only Progression After First-Line Treatment. (J Clin Oncol). Multicentre retrospective cohort of 203 ES-SCLC patients with brain-only progression finding continuing original systemic therapy plus brain RT associated with longer OS2 (14.7 vs ~10 months). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42133905/
- Temporal Trends in Lung Cancer Cases Diagnosed at Early Stage. (JAMA Oncol). US surveillance study of 4.36 million lung cancers showing the share diagnosed at early stage rose from 17.6% (2003) to 30.1% (2022), with a jump around 2016 after screening recommendations. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42133301/
- Spatial and Temporal Analysis Characterizes Evolution of Metastatic Lung Cancer. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery article on spatial and temporal evolution of metastatic lung cancer; no abstract available to judge clinical consequences. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42126004/
- Segmentectomy Versus Lobectomy in NSCLC With Pathologically Invasive Features: A Post Hoc Supplementary Analysis of Multicenter, Phase 3 Trial JCOG0802/WJOG4607L. (J Thorac Oncol). Post hoc analysis of 298 JCOG0802 patients with invasive pathological features showing segmentectomy was not worse than lobectomy for OS (HR 0.66, NS) over 7.1 years. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42119729/
- Advances in Radiological Imaging for Thoracic Oncology: A 20-Year Perspective. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO narrative review of 20 years of thoracic imaging advances (CT, PET, MRI, radiomics) in lung cancer. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42108076/
- Lung Cancer in Morocco. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review mapping gaps in lung cancer diagnostics, molecular testing and drug access in Morocco, relevant to between-country access differences. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42108075/
- A blood-brain barrier-like vascular gate limits immunotherapy efficacy in neuroendocrine cancers. (Cell). Cell preclinical study identifying an ASCL1-IGFBP5-IGF1R blood-brain barrier-like vascular gate in SCLC whose blockade (OSI-906) synergises with anti-PD1 in models. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42102817/
- Rates of Systemic Treatment for Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Among Older Adults. (JAMA Oncol). SEER-Medicare population study of systemic treatment rates and their determinants in patients 65+ with metastatic NSCLC, 2006-2021. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42096214/
- Metastatic Trajectories in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Guide Local and Systemic Therapies. (J Clin Oncol). JCO review proposing a lesion-level 'metastatic trajectories' framework to guide local and systemic therapy in metastatic NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42085620/
- Lung cancer brain metastases management at the dawn of personalized medicine: are we ready to break the barriers? (Ann Oncol). Annals of Oncology review of brain metastasis management in lung cancer combining CNS-penetrant systemic therapy and stereotactic radiotherapy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42061818/
- Evolutionary characterization of lung cancer metastasis. (Nature). Nature TRACERx/PEACE study of 501 samples from 24 NSCLC patients showing metastases diverge genomically and frequently seed further metastases; mechanistic without direct treatment test. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42056508/
- An agentic framework for autonomous scientific discovery in cancer pathology. (Nat Med). Nat Med agentic AI pathology framework (SPARK) evaluated in 18 cohorts including lung adenocarcinoma and squamous carcinoma for prognostic concepts. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42056496/
- Endobronchial Ultrasound-Guided Biopsy-Derived Lung Cancer Models: A Platform for Precision Therapy. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO study establishing 20 stable organoid/PDX models from 175 EBUS needle biopsies with drug responses compared against matched patients. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42055160/
- Integrating Histologic Descriptors Into the Ninth Edition TNM Staging Improves Prognostic Stratification of Lung Adenocarcinoma. (J Thorac Oncol). Two-cohort study (n=1,745) showing grade 3, LVI and STAS add prognostic value to ninth-edition TNM staging in resected lung adenocarcinoma. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42055159/
- Beyond single-target ADCs: are bispecific conjugates the next step in EGFR-mutated NSCLC? (Ann Oncol). Annals of Oncology editorial on bispecific antibody-drug conjugates as a next step in EGFR-mutated NSCLC; opinion piece in a top journal. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42049542/
- First-Line Integration of Local Ablative Therapy With EGFR Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors in Advanced EGFR+ NSCLC: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (J Thorac Oncol). Meta-analysis of 24 comparative studies (6 randomised, 16 retrospective) finding local ablative therapy added to first-line EGFR TKI improved PFS (HR 0.45) and OS (HR 0.52). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42025696/
- Early fibrotic niches establish tumour-permissive microenvironments. (Nature). Nature mouse study showing Kras-mutant alveolar cells use amphiregulin-EGFR signalling in fibroblasts to build a tumour-permissive fibrotic niche; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42020743/
- YAP1 Defines an Emergent, Plastic Population of Relapsed SCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). Analysis of ctDNA, CTCs and biopsies showing an emergent YAP1-positive drug-tolerant population in relapsed SCLC shifting toward LCNEC-like features. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42019833/
- LIF-Induced Tumor Plasticity Establishes an Immunosuppressive Myeloid Niche in LKB1-Mutant Lung Cancer. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery mouse/human study showing LKB1 loss drives LIF-mediated tumour plasticity and immunosuppressive myeloid niche, with LIF neutralisation enhancing T-cell responses. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42008781/
- LAMPAD: An Integrated Circulating Tumor DNA-Based Model for Predicting Potential Cure in Patients With Resected NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). LAMPAD ctDNA model trained on 163 resected NSCLC patients with undetectable landmark MRD, adding preoperative ctDNA to identify patients likely cured. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42002075/
- A Roadmap to Transform Lung Cancer Outcomes: Priorities in Biology, Therapeutic Innovation, Early Detection, Prevention, and Interception. (Cancer Discov). AACR Lung Cancer Task Force roadmap review of research priorities in biology, therapy, early detection and prevention. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42001483/
- Improving immunotherapy in solid tumors using FMT. (Cell). Cell perspective summarising trials of fecal microbiota transplantation to enhance first-line immune checkpoint inhibitors, including in NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41997122/
- Selpercatinib and the Crossover Conundrum: Potential Impact of Postprogression Therapies on Overall Survival. (J Clin Oncol). JCO article (FDA perspective style) on how crossover confounded the immature OS (HR 1.26) in LIBRETTO-431 of selpercatinib in RET-positive NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41996652/
- Stem cell control in the lung by an autocrine injury-activated Igf complex. (Science). Science mouse study identifying an injury-activated Igf2-Igfbp-Rb circuit in lung neuroendocrine stem cells, the cell of origin of SCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41990161/
- Antibody-Drug Conjugates in SCLC: DLL3, B7-H3, TROP2, and Beyond. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO narrative review of DLL3-, B7-H3-, TROP2- and SEZ6-directed ADCs in SCLC with emphasis on early clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41985715/
- GPNMB Drives Brain Metastasis by Sculpting a Pathologic Endothelial-Immune Interactome. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery study showing CTC-secreted GPNMB degrades endothelial EGFR to disrupt the blood-brain barrier and drive brain metastasis in lung cancer and melanoma models. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41973996/
- The Optimization of Radiotherapy in Lung Cancer Treatment: 20 Years of Progress. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of 20 years of radiotherapy progress in NSCLC and SCLC based on key prospective trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41956630/
- Outcomes and Treatments of Patients With Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Who Received Pembrolizumab. (JAMA Oncol). French nationwide cohort describing subsequent treatments and survival after stopping pembrolizumab at 2 years in advanced NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41954917/
- Targeting MET in EGFR-Mutated NSCLC. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of MET amplification/overexpression as EGFR-TKI resistance and the TKI, ADC and bispecific strategies targeting it. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41935667/
- Retinoblastoma-Proficient Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Novel Entity With Therapeutic Vulnerability? (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review on retinoblastoma-proficient SCLC as a possible subgroup sensitive to CDK4/6 inhibition, based largely on preclinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41935666/
- Brief Report: Intestinal Lymphangiectasia With Selpercatinib and Pralsetinib Treatment. (J Thorac Oncol). Retrospective series of 113 RET-inhibitor-treated patients finding radiologic intestinal lymphangiectasia in 29% (5-year cumulative incidence 31%), a long-term toxicity signal. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41933582/
- Lung Cancer in Never Smokers: Genetics, Epidemiology, Environmental Exposures, and Distinct Immune Landscape. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of epidemiology, genetics and screening considerations for lung cancer in never smokers. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41932615/
- ERBB2-Activating Mutations and Co-Occurring Genomic Alterations Contribute to Disease Heterogeneity in Patients With ERBB2-Mutant Lung Cancer. (J Thorac Oncol). GENIE and Flatiron cohort analysis comparing ERBB2 tyrosine kinase domain vs extracellular/transmembrane mutations and chemoimmunotherapy outcomes in ERBB2-mutant NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41932614/
- Ifinatamab deruxtecan, a B7-H3-directed antibody-drug conjugate, in patients with advanced solid tumours (IDeate-PanTumor01): dose-escalation results from a phase 1/2 trial. (Lancet Oncol). Phase 1/2 dose-escalation of B7-H3 ADC ifinatamab deruxtecan in 97 patients across solid tumours including SCLC, ORR 34% overall, with no lung-specific results in the abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41926962/
- NSCLC in Vulnerable and Special Populations: Toward Personalized Care. (J Thorac Oncol). JTO review of NSCLC management in underrepresented populations (young, pregnant, immunocompromised, older adults). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41921747/
- Molecular Profiling Across 80,000 Patients With Lung Cancer. (J Thorac Oncol). Comprehensive genomic profiling of 82,328 NSCLC samples describing actionable alteration distribution across histologies and demographic groups to support universal testing. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41903702/
- Consensus Statement on ctDNA Minimal Residual Disease Testing in Early Stage NSCLC: A Delphi Study by the Asian Thoracic Oncology Research Group. (J Thorac Oncol). ATORG Delphi consensus (27 experts, 23 statements) on ctDNA MRD testing in resected NSCLC, advising against adjuvant de-escalation outside trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41903701/
- Consensus definition of stage III non-small-cell lung cancer technical resectability to standardise inclusion criteria for clinical trials: a multisocietal EORTC-Lung Cancer Group collaboration. (Lancet Respir Med). EORTC-led Delphi consensus (36 experts, 34 statements) defining technical resectability of stage III NSCLC to standardise trial eligibility. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41895314/
- Combination of Radiotherapy and Immunotherapy in Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JAMA Oncol). Hong Kong territory-wide OCEANUS real-world study comparing sequential vs concurrent radiotherapy with immunotherapy in advanced NSCLC using propensity weighting. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41885834/
- The Oncogenic EGFR-SHC1 Fusion Confers Insensitivity to EGFR-TKIs via Dual Activation of N-EGFR Kinase Domain and C-SHC1 Phosphorylation Sites in Lung Cancer. (Cancer Discov). Cancer Discovery study showing EGFR-SHC1 fusions resist EGFR-TKI via SRC-mediated SHC1 phosphorylation, with afatinib plus dasatinib producing regression in one patient. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41874451/
- Implementation of the NHS England Lung Cancer Screening Programme over 5 years. (Nat Med). Nat Med report of the NHS England lung screening programme: over 2 million invited and 7,193 cancers found, 63.1% at stage 1. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41872602/
- Thymic health and immunotherapy outcomes in patients with cancer. (Nature). Nature pan-cancer study of 3,476 ICI-treated patients where CT-derived thymic health was associated with lower progression and mortality risk in NSCLC independent of PD-L1 and TMB. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41851467/
- MTAP loss is frequent in oncogene-driven NSCLC and may confer sensitivity to combined PRMT5 inhibitors and targeted therapies. (Ann Oncol).
